# Integration Test Analysis — CareBridge

- **Project name:** CareBridge SEP490 G79
- **Repository root:** `D:\FU\Term 9\CareBridge_SEP490_G79`
- **Analysis date:** 2026-08-09
- **Analysis mode:** Static, repository-wide integration-test design analysis; no source code or test execution was performed.
- **Required acceptance gate:** Every in-scope automated test must be executed and reach **100% PASS**. A run containing any failed, errored, skipped, disabled, aborted, timed-out, or unexecuted test is not an accepted full-pass result.
- **Main technology stack:** Java 21, Spring Boot 4.1, React 19/Vite 8, Flutter/Dart, FastAPI/Python, Firebase, PostgreSQL, Docker/Nginx/Cloudflare Tunnel.
- **Backend framework:** Spring Boot MVC, Spring Security, Spring Data JPA, Flyway.
- **Frontend frameworks:** React 19 + React Router 7 + TanStack Query; Flutter + GoRouter.
- **Database:** PostgreSQL in runtime; H2, embedded PostgreSQL and Testcontainers appear in tests.
- **Test frameworks detected:** JUnit 5, Mockito, Spring Boot Test/MockMvc, Testcontainers/embedded PostgreSQL, Vitest/Testing Library, Playwright, Flutter Test/Integration Test, pytest/httpx TestClient, Firebase Emulator rules tests, Pester-style PowerShell self-tests, Promptfoo evaluation definitions.

## Scope, evidence, and accuracy boundary

The implementation is treated as the source of truth. Requirements and design files are orientation evidence only: an SRS statement is not used as a pass/fail oracle unless the current implementation, configuration, tests, or an explicitly approved current baseline confirms it. Generated/dependency directories (`node_modules`, build outputs, coverage, `.git`, platform-generated Flutter registrants, historical test-result payloads) were excluded unless a project-owned boundary depended on them.

### SRS freshness and authority audit

Per the repository-owner clarification, an old untouched SRS is stale; a recently committed SRS is only **possibly current**, not automatically authoritative. Git recency was therefore checked against analysis date 2026-08-09 and current HEAD `a92f502a`. The detailed SRS commit `936cf876` is only three days old, but HEAD contains 47 later commits, including 41 commits touching `05_Development`. It is recent evidence, yet it is not synchronized proof of current behavior.

| SRS file | Last Git change | Freshness assessment | Permitted use in this analysis |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `02_Requirements/SRS/3_Functional_Specification_Detailed.md` | 2026-08-06, `936cf876`, added as a 121-UC detailed scope document | Recently committed / possibly new; not confirmed against 41 later source commits | Orientation and scope-gap detection only; implementation wins on conflict |
| `02_Requirements/SRS/3_Functional_Specification_Detailed_5_Non-Functional Requirements__6_Requirement Appendix.md` | 2026-08-06, `936cf876`; document status is `Draft` and its own revised date is 06/08/2026 | Recently committed / possibly new, but still draft and not an approved test oracle | Candidate NFR/gap input only; numeric and scope claims require owner approval or code/config evidence |
| `02_Requirements/SRS/Report1_Project Introduction_new.md` | 2026-08-05, `b77366e9`; file was reintroduced while legacy SRS files were removed | Recently touched, but much of its internal change history/content is from May 2026 | Product-background context only |
| `02_Requirements/SRS/Report2_Project Management Plan.docx.md` | 2026-08-05, `b77366e9`; that commit changed only a small part of this plan | Recently touched does not prove the full management plan is current | Planning background only; not a functional/NFR oracle |

There is a concrete same-commit conflict: `3_Functional_Specification_Detailed.md` lists MF-13 connected device/health-data integration in the current release, while the draft appendix says connected health devices are excluded (`FS-06`, `NFR-DI-05`). Current code implements phone sensor/camera/location flows but no wearable or connected-medical-device interface. Consequently, wearable release scope is **Needs manual verification**; this report states only the implementation fact and does not choose either SRS claim.

The scan found 422 Java HTTP method/path rows (including both `POST` and `PATCH` on the shared reminder `skip` handler), five FastAPI routes in the AI triage service, two FastAPI routes in the exercise-correction sidecar, 14 scheduled jobs, and multiple after-commit/domain-event listeners. The code graph contained 3,388 analyzed project files, 24,490 code nodes, 430 endpoint nodes including test-only probes, and 936 execution flows. The endpoint inventory below filters out test-only endpoints.

Existing automated source tests detected:

| Suite | Test source files | Actual execution character |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Backend Java | 721 | 19 files declare `@SpringBootTest`, 103 declare MVC slices, 139 files directly use or inherit the PostgreSQL/container integration base, and 429 are mock-heavy; filename alone is not used as the classification |
| Web | 31 unit/component + 5 Playwright E2E | Primarily mocked UI/API units; five browser flows |
| Mobile | 160 unit/widget + 3 `integration_test` | Mostly widget/service contract tests; three app-level flows |
| AI triage | 50 pytest files | Algorithm/API contract coverage; Gemini and evidence dependencies are normally stubbed/mocked |
| Firebase rules | 1 | Emulator-oriented Firestore authorization rules |
| AI evaluation | 16 pytest files | Evaluation harness, datasets, adapters and reporting rather than product E2E |
| Test automation support | 8 tracked PowerShell runners/scripts | 3 OV-01 evidence/quality-gate files plus 5 database/mobile deployment rehearsal runners |

## Integration architecture summary

`React/Flutter -> Spring Security filters -> Controller -> domain service/policy -> JPA repository -> PostgreSQL` is the main path. Cross-cutting branches publish audit records, domain events, outbox jobs, FCM notifications, Firebase conversation signals, and calls to Gemini, TrackAsia, Cloudinary/R2, CompreFace, ZegoCloud, SMTP, or the Python AI/exercise services. Firebase is used for federated identity verification, FCM, and direct-conversation signaling; the durable source of truth for conversations and most domain state remains PostgreSQL.

No project-owned WebSocket server implementation was found. Realtime conversation/call signaling is implemented through Firebase/Firestore listeners and ZegoCloud room sessions.

# Testability and Risk Assessment

## Actionable testability concerns

| ID | Concern | Impact on integration testing | Required action / owner / timeline |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TST-01 | Runtime PostgreSQL/Flyway differs from the default H2 test profile; only a minority of tests exercise real PostgreSQL semantics. | Locks, partial indexes, JSON, constraints and migration behavior can pass H2 but fail in deployment. | Backend/QA: make PostgreSQL Testcontainers the canonical integration profile before release-candidate gating. |
| TST-02 | Most external adapters are verified with mocks; credentials and disposable sandbox endpoints are not consistently available in CI. | Contract drift and provider-specific failure shapes remain undetected. | Platform/QA: provision sandbox secrets and deterministic WireMock/provider-contract suites before external integration sign-off. |
| TST-03 | Only five web E2E and three mobile integration tests exist for a very large route/API surface. | Client/server DTO drift, route guards, token refresh, upload, notifications and cross-role flows can regress unnoticed. | QA/Web/Mobile: add risk-driven API and thin E2E coverage in the next test milestone. |
| TST-04 | Scheduled jobs and after-commit listeners use clocks, delays, asynchronous execution and outbox state across many modules. | Flaky waits, duplicate effects and unobserved retry failures are likely without controllable time and job triggers. | Backend: expose test-only clock/job controls through test configuration, not production endpoints, before job integration suites. |
| TST-05 | Firebase, FCM and Zego require multiple emulators/sandboxes and asynchronous assertions. | Parallel tests can collide or leave data behind. | QA/Platform: namespace users/conversations/tokens per run and provide cleanup fixtures before CI parallelization. |
| TST-06 | The recent-but-unverified draft SRS marks latency, capacity, availability, MTTR, browser floor and accessibility contrast targets `Open`; current code/config also supplies no approved numeric oracle. | A final NFR pass/fail oracle cannot be defined. | Product/Architecture: approve and version the thresholds before performance/accessibility release gates. |
| TST-07 | A dev-only `GET /api/manual-seed` endpoint mutates data and is permitted by the catch-all security rule when the dev profile/property enable it. | Misconfigured environments could expose destructive/non-idempotent seed behavior. | Backend/DevOps: integration-test profile gating and verify the bean is absent in staging/prod before deployment. |
| TST-08 | Tracked runtime configuration contains CompreFace API-key literals. | Secret rotation/leak risk and inability to safely reproduce identity-provider tests. | Security/DevOps: rotate and externalize keys immediately; verify no secret value enters logs or reports. |
| TST-09 | Some Flutter mother/baby routes are declared outside the client `ProtectedRoute` concept used on web; mobile protection depends on router/state and server enforcement. | Direct navigation may show protected shells before API rejection. | Mobile/QA: add unauthenticated deep-link integration tests; server remains the authorization authority. |
| TST-10 | Historical test outputs are extensive, but there is no single current repository-wide green-run artifact for this merged state. | Static coverage cannot prove the merged branch currently runs successfully. | QA/CI: produce a clean, timestamped full-suite result after this analysis. |
| TST-11 | SRS files were committed on 2026-08-05/06, but 47 commits (41 touching implementation) followed; the two detailed files also conflict on connected-device scope. | Tests derived directly from SRS may validate stale or mutually incompatible behavior. | Product/BA/Architecture: reconcile the SRS against HEAD, resolve MF-13 versus `FS-06`/`NFR-DI-05`, and tag an approved requirement baseline. |

## What is already strong

- Controllers, services, repositories and external adapters are separated well enough to replace provider boundaries.
- Security is stateless and server-side method security is widely used.
- Many high-risk data flows already have concurrency, migration, security or PostgreSQL-focused tests.
- AI triage has deterministic red-flag rules, parity vectors, dataset integrity hashes and explicit safe-degradation logic.
- Notification, consultation and direct-chat flows commonly use after-commit events/outbox jobs to avoid sending effects before persistence commits.
- Stable error codes are concentrated in typed exceptions and a global error envelope.
- Firebase rules have a dedicated emulator test and direct-conversation code deliberately collapses cross-conversation “not found” states to avoid existence leaks.

| Reliability | No duplicate effects; external/sensor failures preserve consistent safe state. | Behavioral target exists; availability and MTTR are **UNKNOWN/Open**. | PostgreSQL idempotency/concurrency tests, job burn-in, provider fault injection, monitoring logs. |
| Performance | Bounded pagination/lazy loading; AI/map must show timeout/retry guidance. | p95/p99 latency, concurrency and capacity are **UNKNOWN/Open**. | Approved k6 baseline, DB query plans, client timing evidence. |

| Safety/compliance | No diagnosis/dispatch claims; RED/fall flows keep urgent actions visible; uncertainty defaults safer. | Qualitative oracle exists; clinical review remains required. | Dataset/evaluation suite, cross-service integration, copy/golden accessibility review. |
| Maintainability | Backend, web and mobile builds/tests are quality gates; bidirectional traceability. | No current all-suite result for this merge. | CI result, coverage report, this traceability report, static analysis. |
| Compatibility/accessibility | Safe degradation when sensor/camera/location unavailable. | Browser floor and contrast target are **UNKNOWN/Open**. | Device/browser matrix, permission-change integration, accessibility scanner/manual evidence. |

## Proposed execution and quality gates

P0/P1/P2/P3 below mean risk priority, not execution timing. Run all deterministic functional suites in pull requests if total duration stays below 15 minutes; move provider-sandbox, performance, burn-in and chaos suites to nightly/weekly schedules.

- Required pass rate for P0, P1, P2 and P3 tests: **100%**.
- Required final counts: **0 failed, 0 errors, 0 skipped, 0 disabled, 0 aborted, 0 timed out, and 0 unexecuted in-scope tests**.
- A test or scenario may be labeled `PASS` only when a current, reproducible execution artifact for the analyzed commit proves it passed; historical results and static inspection are not pass evidence.
- Provider-, device-, clinical-, or environment-dependent scenarios remain `Needs manual verification` until they are actually executed successfully in the required environment; they must not be silently excluded to obtain a green percentage.
- No open defect that exposes data, fabricates medical/emergency state, loses confirmed safety evidence, or breaks a high-risk mitigation.
- Every high-risk item (score ≥6) must have implemented mitigation evidence.
- Target at least 80% of identified integration scenarios automated; final NFR PASS/CONCERNS/FAIL belongs to a later evidence assessment.
- Estimated QA automation effort: P0 ~4–7 weeks, P1 ~6–10 weeks, P2 ~4–8 weeks, P3 ~1–3 weeks; total ~15–28 QA-engineer weeks, assuming reusable factories, PostgreSQL/Firebase environments and provider stubs are ready.

# Feature Analysis

## Identity & Security

### Feature: Account registration, OTP verification and federated authentication

**Module:**
Identity & Security

**Feature purpose:**
Create local Mother/Family/Expert-facing identities, verify OTP challenges, and exchange a verified Firebase federated identity for a CareBridge account/session.

**Actors / Roles:**
Guest; newly registered user; Firebase identity provider; email service.

**Entry points:**
`POST /api/v1/auth/register`, `/verify-otp`, `/resend-otp`, `/federated`; web/mobile registration and federated-login screens.

**Integration flow:**
`React/Flutter -> AuthController -> AuthServiceImpl/OtpServiceImpl/FederatedAuthServiceImpl -> UserRepository/OtpVerificationRepository/UserSessionRepository -> PostgreSQL`; OTP email goes through `EmailService`; federated tokens go through `FirebaseAdminTokenVerifier`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/controller/AuthController.java` — public HTTP entry points.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/impl/AuthServiceImpl.java` — local registration, OTP and token/session orchestration.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/impl/FederatedAuthServiceImpl.java` — federated account/link decision logic.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/federation/FirebaseAdminTokenVerifier.java` — Firebase Admin token verification.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/auth/services/firebaseAuth.ts` — browser-side federated acquisition.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/lib/features/auth/services/auth_service.dart` — mobile federated exchange.

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/RegistrationIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/VerifyOtpIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/OtpRaceConditionIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/FederatedRegistrationIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/e2e/federated-emulator.spec.ts`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/auth/federated_registration_test.dart`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`users`, `auth_challenges`, `auth_sessions`; refresh-token/session hash persistence.

**External integrations:**
SMTP/Gmail email; Firebase Authentication/Admin SDK.

**Preconditions:**
Unique normalized email/phone, supported role, valid request fields; valid non-expired OTP or Firebase ID token when applicable.

**Main success scenario:**
Registration persists a pending/appropriate user and challenge, sends OTP, verification activates the account, and authentication creates an access token plus durable refresh/session state. Federated authentication verifies provider identity before creating/linking a CareBridge identity.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Blank/invalid email or Vietnamese phone, weak/invalid password, duplicate account, unsupported role/provider, wrong/expired/already-consumed OTP, resend/attempt rate limit, invalid/revoked Firebase token, provider email conflict, concurrent OTP consumption.

**Expected system behavior:**
Validation failures use the common 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` envelope; authentication failures return 401 `AUTHENTICATION_FAILED`; rate limits return 429 `RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`; duplicates/conflicts return the typed business status/code. Failed requests must not create a second user/session or consume a valid proof incorrectly.

**Important edge cases:**
Two verifications of the same OTP; two federated requests for the same identity; email send failure after challenge creation; normalized phone duplicates; provider token valid but CareBridge account suspended.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Spring integration/service tests cover registration, verification, resend and OTP races; federated service/controller/mobile/web tests cover several emulator and failure contracts. Firebase provider calls are mostly mocked/emulated.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
One disposable full flow spanning real PostgreSQL + SMTP sandbox + Firebase emulator + both clients; rollback/compensation when email delivery fails; complete role matrix and concurrent federated linking.

### Feature: Login, refresh rotation, logout, password recovery and session management

**Module:**
Identity & Security

**Feature purpose:**
Authenticate local credentials, issue/rotate tokens, enforce account state, revoke sessions, change/reset passwords and list/revoke a user’s active sessions.

**Actors / Roles:**
Guest for login/refresh/forgot/reset; any authenticated user for logout/change password/session management.

**Entry points:**
`POST /api/v1/auth/login`, `/refresh`, `/logout`, `/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`, `/change-password`; `GET/DELETE /api/v1/sessions...`.

**Integration flow:**
`Client -> JwtAuthenticationFilter/AuthController/SessionController -> AuthServiceImpl/SessionService/ForgotPasswordServiceImpl/ResetPasswordServiceImpl -> users/auth_sessions/auth_challenges -> PostgreSQL`; recovery emails use SMTP.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/controller/AuthController.java` — authentication/password endpoints.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/impl/AuthServiceImpl.java` — login, refresh rotation and logout.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/policy/AuthenticationPolicy.java` — disabled/locked/suspended account enforcement.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/jwt/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java` — protected-request authentication.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/identity/controller/SessionController.java` — session list/revocation API.

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/LoginIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/AuthServiceRefreshTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/LogoutIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/filter/JwtAuthenticationFilterAccountStateTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/identity/service/impl/SessionServiceImplTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/core/network/api_client_session_race_test.dart`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`users`, `auth_sessions`, `auth_challenges`; token hashes/blacklist/session revocation state; client secure token storage.

**External integrations:**
SMTP/Gmail for password reset; device/user-agent metadata only.

**Preconditions:**
Existing active account and correct password; unexpired non-revoked refresh/reset proof; authenticated subject owns target session.

**Main success scenario:**
Login checks rate/account policy and password, clears stale current-session flags, writes a session/refresh hash and returns tokens/profile. Refresh atomically revokes/rotates the old proof. Logout or session revoke invalidates durable state; password reset/change revokes appropriate sessions.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Unknown/wrong credentials, invalid identifier, temporary/admin lock, disabled/suspended account, expired/revoked/replayed refresh token, session not found/not owned, wrong current password, invalid/expired reset proof, concurrent refresh/logout.

**Expected system behavior:**
401 codes include `AUTHENTICATION_FAILED`, `INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN`, `SESSION_REVOKED`; 403 includes `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, `ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED`, `ACCOUNT_ADMIN_LOCKED`, `ACCOUNT_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED`; 404 `SESSION_NOT_FOUND`. Token replay must not create another valid session.

**Important edge cases:**
Refresh and logout racing; two refreshes using the same token; password reset while another device refreshes; JWT cryptographically valid after account state changes; revoke-all excluding current session.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Login/refresh/logout and filter tests exercise many policies, with some Spring integration coverage; mobile API-client tests exercise local token races. Many repository/provider boundaries remain mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
One multi-device, real-DB race suite through HTTP/security filters; SMTP recovery end-to-end; access-token rejection immediately after account/admin/session state mutation.

### Feature: Profile, privacy, consent and own-account lifecycle

**Module:**
Identity & Security

**Feature purpose:**
Read/update user profile and privacy preferences, grant/revoke data permissions, link identities and deactivate the current account.

**Actors / Roles:**
Any authenticated user; Mother/Family/Expert where ownership/consent policies apply.

**Entry points:**
`GET/PUT /api/v1/profile`, `GET/PUT /api/v1/privacy-settings`, `GET/POST/DELETE /api/v1/consent/grants...`, authenticated profile/identity/deactivate endpoints under `/api/v1/auth`.

**Integration flow:**
`Client -> ProfileController/PrivacySettingsController/ConsentController/AuthController -> services -> UserRepository/ConsentGrantRepository -> PostgreSQL -> audit/event consumers`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/profile/controller/ProfileController.java` — profile API.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/privacy/controller/PrivacySettingsController.java` — privacy API.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/consent/controller/ConsentController.java` — consent grant/list/revoke API.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/consent/service/impl/ConsentServiceImpl.java` — permission lifecycle.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/impl/AuthServiceImpl.java` — deactivation and identity profile operations.

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/profile/ProfileIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/privacy/controller/PrivacySettingsControllerTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/consent/ConsentRevocationLocationShareConcurrencyPostgresTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/AuthServiceDeactivateTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/shared/api/apiClient.test.ts`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`users`, `data_permissions`, `auth_sessions`, audit events; profile fields stored with user data.

**External integrations:**
Firebase identity link/lookup for Google identity operations.

**Preconditions:**
Valid JWT, active account, target permission scope and grantee exist; actor owns the profile or is explicitly authorized.

**Main success scenario:**
Updates persist only allowed profile/privacy fields. Consent grants create a scoped permission and revocation disables future access/location sharing. Deactivation changes account state and invalidates sessions.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Invalid profile format, unsupported permission/scope, duplicate or missing grant, self/ownership mismatch, revoked consent, concurrent location read during revoke, invalid Firebase identity link, deactivated account reuse.

**Expected system behavior:**
Invalid DTOs return 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`; consent denial returns 403 `CONSENT_DENIED`; absent resources return 404. Revocation must commit atomically with access denial and no future location collection.

**Important edge cases:**
Grant/revoke races, previously cached shared data, deactivation with pending jobs/conversations, identity link conflicts, retention of required audit/moderation/safety evidence.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Profile has Spring integration plus DTO/service tests; consent includes real PostgreSQL concurrency tests; privacy controller/service tests are mostly slice/unit.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Cross-module revocation over health summaries, family dashboard, expert location and files; deactivation-to-token/FCM/Firebase cleanup; data-retention policy verification.

### Feature: Administrative identity governance, audit, security incidents and maintenance configuration

**Module:**
Identity & Security

**Feature purpose:**
Allow privileged staff to search/manage users and roles, create staff, review audit/security events, and change system/maintenance settings.

**Actors / Roles:**
`SYSTEM_ADMIN`, `OPERATIONS`; selected audit endpoints allow both; other users forbidden.

**Entry points:**
Admin endpoints under `/api/v1/admin/users`, `/roles`, `/staff`, `/audit-logs`, `/security-incidents`, `/system-configuration`; `MaintenanceModeFilter`.

**Integration flow:**
`Admin web -> Spring Security/@PreAuthorize -> admin/audit/system controllers -> services -> users/audit_events/system_configurations -> PostgreSQL -> maintenance filter behavior`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/identity/admin/controller/AdminUserController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/identity/admin/service/AdminUserService.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/audit/controller/AuditController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/audit/controller/SecurityIncidentController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/systemconfiguration/controller/SystemConfigurationController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/systemconfiguration/security/MaintenanceModeFilter.java`

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/identity/admin/controller/AdminUserControllerIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/audit/controller/AuditControllerTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/audit/controller/SecurityIncidentControllerTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/systemconfiguration/MaintenanceModeFilterTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/admin/pages/UpdateUserRolePage.test.tsx`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/aiRuleManagement/pages/SystemConfigurationPage.test.tsx`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`users`, `audit_events`, `system_configurations`, account/session/security state.

**External integrations:**
None required for core persistence; downstream tokens/clients observe changed account/maintenance state.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated privileged role, valid target user/role/status, configuration key and version/state accepted.

**Main success scenario:**
Admin mutation persists governance state, writes audit evidence, and subsequent protected requests observe the new role/account/maintenance decision.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Unauthenticated 401, wrong role 403 `ACCESS_DENIED`, target not found, invalid role transition, self-lock/unsafe admin action where prohibited, stale configuration update, audit write failure, maintenance exemptions.

**Expected system behavior:**
Unauthorized data is not returned. Required audited changes must roll back if required audit persistence fails. Maintenance mode returns the configured blocked response for non-exempt paths without blocking readiness/auth behavior intended by the filter.

**Important edge cases:**
Role update during active JWT session, concurrent admin decisions, pagination and filtering, audit log containing secrets/health payload, last-system-admin protection (needs manual verification where not explicit).

**Existing automated coverage:**
MVC/security/service tests are broad; repository and one admin-user integration test exist; required-audit rollback has dedicated tests elsewhere.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Full web-to-real-DB admin workflow; changed role/account state through an already-issued JWT; maintenance behavior through reverse proxy and mobile/web clients.

Authenticated Mother, valid lifecycle dates/stage, owned active journey; required consent/context for dependent operations.

**Main success scenario:**
Onboarding persists canonical journey context, a valid transition changes status/stage once, writes audit/event data, and timeline/recommendations reflect the committed state.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Missing/invalid dates, impossible stage/outcome transition, duplicate active journey, unauthorized family mutation, stale/replayed transition, consent revoked, audit failure.

Invalid transitions return typed 400/409 behavior; no partial downstream state is visible before commit. After-commit listeners distribute recommendations/checklists only after the journey transaction succeeds.

End-to-end mobile journey transition with real PostgreSQL plus checklist/recommendation/event verification; rollback when downstream/audit processing fails.

Exercise-correction FastAPI sidecar; device camera; browser JS bridge on web-capable Flutter.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated Mother, active exercise, cleared safety check, configured/reachable model, required landmarks and supported exercise key, camera permission.

**Main success scenario:**
A session starts once, valid landmark batches receive posture feedback, events/results persist, and completion produces history/result data.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Exercise/config not found, inactive exercise, safety not cleared, duplicate/invalid session transition, not owner, unsupported exercise (400 `UNSUPPORTED_EXERCISE`), invalid input or missing landmarks (422 `INVALID_INPUT`/`MISSING_LANDMARKS`), model unavailable (503 `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`), timeout.

**Expected system behavior:**
Provider failure maps to a controlled unavailable/error result and must not mark an incomplete session successful. Model errors retain stable code/message; persistence remains consistent.

**Important edge cases:**
Partial landmark batches, session pause/resume, ML timeout after DB update, camera permission revoked mid-session, duplicate completion, offline feedback buffering.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Backend controller/service/adapter tests and mobile analyzer/camera contracts are broad; ML endpoint errors are implemented with stable responses.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
A disposable backend+ML HTTP suite using real JSON and PostgreSQL; device camera-to-sidecar flow; timeout/circuit behavior and partial persistence.

`community_content`, `community_interactions`, `community_topics`, attachments, notification records.

Cloudinary for public community images; Gemini AI moderation queue; FCM reply notifications.

**Preconditions:**
Valid JWT, visible topic, ownership for edits/deletes, approved eligible expert for restricted answers, valid body/image.

**Main success scenario:**
A valid question/answer persists, becomes visible according to moderation state, interactions are unique per actor/target, and notifications/scans are created after commit.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Blank/oversized body, topic missing/hidden/invalid hierarchy, duplicate topic, locked/non-answerable question, not owner/not editable, expert not eligible, duplicate like/bookmark, invalid image, content awaiting moderation.

**Expected system behavior:**
Defined community codes include `COM-001`, `COM-003`, `COM-006`, `COM-007`, `COM-009`–`COM-017` with 400/404/409/422 as mapped by `GlobalExceptionHandler`. Unauthorized requests receive 401/403 without private fields.

**Important edge cases:**
Anonymous-display projection, image upload succeeds but post fails, moderation races with edit/delete, interaction counts under concurrent toggles, reply notification duplication.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Many MVC/service/security tests, topic and image integration tests, plus mobile widget/service contracts. Full external calls are mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Real DB + storage + AI scan + FCM after-commit flow; anonymity/privacy projections; concurrency for interactions and moderation/edit races.

Community & Moderation

**Feature purpose:**
Create reports on content/accounts; allow moderators to claim/release/review/resolve reports, hide/lock content, warn/suspend/restrict users, undo eligible actions and preserve evidence.

**Actors / Roles:**
Authenticated reporter; `MODERATOR`; `SYSTEM_ADMIN` for escalation/selected decisions.

**Entry points:**
`/api/v1/reports` and admin/moderation queue, pending-content, history, actions, account-actions, claim/release/resolve/undo endpoints.

**Integration flow:**
`Client -> ReportController/ModerationController/SystemAdminEscalationController -> services/policies -> moderation_cases/community_content/users/audit_events -> PostgreSQL -> notification/security state`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/ReportController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/ModerationController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/service/ModerationService.java`

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/report/ReportIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/moderation/UndoModerationActionIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/ModerateContentControllerSecurityTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/expert/pages/ExpertVerificationQueuePage.test.tsx`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`moderation_cases`, `community_content`, `users`, `audit_events`, notification records.

**External integrations:**
FCM/in-app notifications where configured; AI assessment evidence can feed the queue.

**Preconditions:**
Existing reportable target; reporter is not target owner; moderator role; claim/current-state rules satisfied.

**Main success scenario:**
Report is uniquely created, moderator claims it atomically, applies a supported action, resolves the report, changes target/account visibility/state, writes audit evidence and notifies affected users.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Target missing (`RPT-002`), report rate limit (`RPT-003`), duplicate pending (`RPT-004`), own target (`RPT-005`), unsupported action, missing reason/expiry, self-action, already resolved, claimed by another moderator, stale undo/superseded status.

**Expected system behavior:**
Typed 400/403/404/409/429 responses use the exact `RPT-*`/`MOD-*` codes. Claim/action/resolve must be atomic and preserve evidence; failed/stale actions must not alter target state.

**Important edge cases:**
Two moderators claim simultaneously, new action supersedes undo, target deleted while report pending, suspension observed by an active JWT, rollback when audit write fails.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Controller security and many service/integration/concurrency tests cover moderation cases. Browser coverage for full moderator workflow is sparse.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Web UI through real Spring Security/PostgreSQL; report-to-FCM/account-filter propagation; all action/undo concurrency combinations.

Moderation enabled/configured, valid active policy, queued target version, Gemini credentials or safe disabled mode.

**Main success scenario:**
One job claims a queued item, Gemini response is parsed and policy-applied, assessment is persisted, and the target is auto-handled or placed in human review with audit trace.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Missing/disabled configuration, Gemini timeout/429/5xx, malformed/unsafe JSON, unknown target/version, duplicate job, claim race, stale policy, parser failure, moderator role violation.

**Expected system behavior:**
No unsafe response is accepted silently. Job failure records retry/failure state without duplicating target action. Privileged endpoints return 401/403 for unauthorized users.

**Important edge cases:**
Content edited while scan runs, two workers claim same job, provider succeeds after client timeout, policy change between enqueue and apply, prompt/log redaction.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Worker, policy, parser, client and security tests are extensive; one Spring atomic-claim integration test exists. Real Gemini test is environment-dependent.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Deterministic provider-stub HTTP contract through worker and PostgreSQL; retry/dead-letter behavior; end-to-end moderator web review of an AI-created case.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated Expert/application identity, valid profile/specialty/credential metadata, authorized file ownership/purpose, configured provider for face verification; admin role for review.

**Main success scenario:**
Applicant data and protected documents persist, identity verification evaluates provider results, admin review atomically changes expert status, and only approved experts become directory/consultation eligible.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Invalid profile/credential fields, duplicate identity/credential, unsupported or oversized file, missing/foreign attachment, face not detected/multiple faces/mismatch, provider timeout/config error, invalid review transition, wrong role, stale concurrent review.

**Expected system behavior:**
Failed provider or validation must not mark an expert verified. Private documents are returned only through authorized file access. Review produces durable status plus audit; unauthorized requests return 401/403.

**Important edge cases:**
Upload succeeds but application fails, credential deleted during review, key rotation, two admins decide simultaneously, approved expert later suspended, provider response at threshold boundary.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Profile/validation/service tests, identity/duplicate-face/preview tests, and web/mobile onboarding tests exist; CompreFace is mocked and no sandbox integration test was found.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Real PostgreSQL + R2-compatible store + CompreFace stub/sandbox pipeline; concurrent review; document access after status/account changes; secret/config failure profile.

**External integrations:**
FCM; AI/evidence data as handoff input.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated Mother, target Expert approved/eligible, valid preferred window and client request ID; triage context owned and permitted; Expert is assigned responder.

**Main success scenario:**
Request persists once, after-commit event notifies Expert, accept uses a conditional/locked transition, creates/reuses the direct conversation and returns updated state. Handoff shares only approved context/citations.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Expert not found/ineligible (`CONREQ-002/004/006`), request missing (`CONREQ-007`), no longer pending (`CONREQ-005`), idempotency conflict (`CONREQ-009`), invalid window, wrong actor, expired request, context not owned/consented, concurrent accept/reject.

**Expected system behavior:**
Typed 404/409 responses leave request/conversation unchanged. Exactly one competing response wins; notification is published only after commit and should not duplicate.

**Important edge cases:**
Expert becomes ineligible after request creation, accept races expiry/reject, same clientRequestId with different payload, notification failure after persistence, handoff revoke after conversation starts.

**Existing automated coverage:**
API, migration, expiry, concurrency and PostgreSQL handoff tests are substantial; listener and mobile contracts exist.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Real FCM/emulator outbox delivery; full Mother-to-Expert cross-client flow; consent revoke and citation/file access after handoff; retry/dead-letter assertions.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated participant, eligible Expert, active conversation, valid body/clientMessageId/cursor, attachment belongs to conversation, valid call actor/state, configured provider.

**Main success scenario:**
Message commits once, timeline returns ordered participant-only data, after-commit signal/notification is emitted, and an authorized call state transition returns time-limited Zego join credentials.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Not Mother to start (`DCC-001`), Expert ineligible (`DCC-002`), not participant (`DCC-003`), body outside 1–2000 (`DCC-004`), idempotency conflict (`DCC-005`), not found/existence-collapsed (`DCC-006`), invalid call state (`DCC-007`), Zego failure (`DCC-008`), wrong actor (`DCC-009`), invalid cursor (`DCC-011`), Firebase unavailable (`DCC-012`).

**Expected system behavior:**
Unauthorized actors cannot distinguish foreign-resource existence. Duplicate same-content client ID is idempotent; different content conflicts. Firebase/Zego failure is controlled and does not corrupt durable conversation/call state.

**Important edge cases:**
Message commit then signal/FCM failure, concurrent send/read, cursor at deletion boundary, call timeout versus accept, Expert approval revoked mid-conversation, Firestore retention cleanup.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Real-DB concurrency/read-boundary tests, controller/policy/job tests, Firebase/Zego unit contracts and web/mobile call reducers are broad; external providers are not exercised together.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
One PostgreSQL + Firebase emulator + FCM + Zego sandbox cross-client flow; attachment authorization through chat; event replay/retention and provider outage recovery.

**External integrations:**
Python AI triage service; Gemini when enabled; emergency/map and notification downstream.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated owner, canonical target entity/stage, accepted triage consent where required, valid symptom/answers/session state, service credential between backend and AI service.

**Main success scenario:**
Start creates/reuses an owned session, deterministic safety rules run before generative processing, response persists with GREEN/YELLOW/RED outcome, and committed RED/YELLOW outcomes trigger emergency/follow-up consumers.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Missing/conflicting stage (Python 422 “A canonical triage stage is required” / “Conflicting triage stages”), invalid/foreign/expired/completed session, missing target, consent absent, malformed AI response, timeout/unavailable service, red flag despite AI green, duplicate continuation.

**Expected system behavior:**
RED preemption cannot be downgraded by Gemini. Incomplete/uncertain input defaults to cautious YELLOW or asks more. Failure leaves a controlled non-diagnostic response/state and never fabricates citations/emergency dispatch.

**Important edge cases:**
Retry after timeout when provider actually completed, continuation from another device, lifecycle transition mid-session, exact red-rule parity between Java and Python, event listener failure after commit.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Backend Spring/embedded PostgreSQL and extensive AI pytest parity/safety/state tests exist; mobile continuation and UI contracts are strong. Most cross-service HTTP is mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Full Flutter→Java→Python→PostgreSQL→emergency/reminder path; AI timeout/retry idempotency; consent revocation and multi-device continuation; provider malformed payload.

### Feature: Gemini RAG, extraction and safe fallback

**Module:**
AI Triage & Gemini

**Feature purpose:**
Use approved CareBridge content/context to answer or enrich guidance with Gemini while applying stage, citation, safety and non-diagnostic policies.

**Actors / Roles:**
Authenticated end user through RAG endpoint; backend services; Gemini provider.

**Entry points:**
RAG endpoint, Gemini extraction/moderation/triage clients, care-journey insight service.

**Integration flow:**
`API/service -> RagPolicyService -> context retriever -> safety filter -> GeminiHttpClient -> response parser/citation policy -> response`; fallback implementation is selected when provider is unavailable/disabled.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/integration/gemini/controller/RagController.java`

**Database / Storage involved:**
Approved `content_items`, knowledge/evidence sources and optional health/journey context; no Gemini response should become authoritative clinical data by itself.

**External integrations:**
Google Gemini API.

**Preconditions:**
Provider enabled/key present or fallback configured, approved context available, valid stage/audience/request, safety filters pass.

**Main success scenario:**
System retrieves only approved context, sends a minimized prompt, validates provider response/citations and returns safe, non-diagnostic guidance with source metadata.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Missing config, timeout/429/5xx, malformed JSON/text, absent/invalid citations, prompt injection, red-flag content, stage mismatch, no approved context, provider success after caller timeout.

**Expected system behavior:**
Provider/config failures map to typed `RagException`/unavailable or safe fallback; no fabricated citation or diagnostic claim is returned; logs redact prompts/secrets as required.

**Important edge cases:**
Citation content unpublished during request, fallback/profile selection drift, retry duplication/cost, context exceeding token limit, Vietnamese wording preservation.

**Existing automated coverage:**
RAG policy, context, startup-without-Gemini, fallback/log-redaction and safety tests are broad; the real provider test is environment-dependent.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Deterministic stub-server contract at HTTP serialization boundary; sandbox canary with approved dataset; timeout/cost/rate-limit behavior across backend and Python clients.

**Main success scenario:**
Nearby query returns authorized/verified facilities ordered by supported distance logic; provider route/place data is labeled with source/status and shown on the client.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Missing/out-of-range coordinates, invalid radius/pagination, facility not found/unverified, location permission denied, TrackAsia timeout/4xx/5xx/malformed/empty response, admin wrong role.

**Expected system behavior:**
Invalid request returns controlled 400; provider failure yields safe fallback/unavailable state and manual emergency guidance, never a fabricated route, ETA or verification result.

**Preconditions:**
Owned subject/journey, valid record type/content/time, actor has current scoped permission for shared reads.

**Main success scenario:**
Owner mutation persists; owner summary aggregates correct records; shared response includes only fields/scope allowed by active consent and membership.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Invalid record/metric/date, missing subject, duplicate/reference conflict, foreign owner, permission missing/revoked/expired, archived subject, attachment not authorized.

**Expected system behavior:**
Unauthorized reads return 403/404 according to anti-enumeration policy and no health fields leak. Revoked consent removes future shared results without deleting required owner records.

**Important edge cases:**
Permission revoked during paged read, family member removed, summary cache/staleness, deletion/retention with audit, attachment access separate from record access.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Service, metric and family sharing/contract tests exist; many use mocks and only selected repository paths use real PostgreSQL.

Parameterized cross-role field-level privacy through HTTP+PostgreSQL; consent/member revocation races; health record plus private attachment authorization.

Health Records & Storage

**Database / Storage involved:**
`attachments`; Cloudinary public storage; R2 S3-compatible private bucket. Runtime multipart limit is 5 MB/file and 16 MB/request.

**External integrations:**
Cloudinary API; Cloudflare R2/S3 SDK; Apache Tika content inspection.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated/authorized actor, declared supported purpose, non-empty allowed file, size within limits, related target ownership/role.

**Main success scenario:**
File is validated by content/purpose, uploaded to the correct provider, metadata persists, and authorized view/delete resolves the object without exposing raw private storage URLs.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Empty file, invalid MIME/magic/type, per-file/request size exceeded, unsupported purpose, foreign target/file, provider timeout/partial failure, metadata save failure after upload, delete provider failure, path/key injection.

**Expected system behavior:**
Invalid multipart returns 400/413 as configured by Spring/container; no metadata is created for rejected files. Storage/DB failure must compensate or leave detectable cleanup state. Private file access returns 403/404 without URL leakage.

**Important edge cases:**
Same filename, provider upload succeeds then DB rollback, DB delete then provider failure, signed URL expiry, orphan cleanup racing with content publish, chat attachment reused across conversations.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Controller/service/policy/provider unit tests and cleanup job tests cover many branches; providers are mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
MinIO/R2-compatible and Cloudinary sandbox HTTP suites; multipart through reverse proxy; transaction compensation/orphan reconciliation; cross-feature access matrix.

Firebase Cloud Messaging; Firebase app bootstrap.

**Main success scenario:**
Current token is upserted to the user/device, preferences persist, notification list is ownership-filtered, and read action updates the target record.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Blank/oversized token, invalid platform, token bound to another user/device, foreign notification, record not found, duplicate registration, token refresh/race, Firebase unavailable.

**Expected system behavior:**
Unauthorized requests return 401/403; duplicate token registration is idempotent or updates ownership according to service policy; foreign notification content is never returned.

**Important edge cases:**
Logout without deregistration, same physical token reused after account switch, permission revoked at OS level, token rotation while delivery runs, unread counts under concurrent mark-read.

Backend controller/service/preference tests and mobile FCM/routing tests cover local behavior; FCM itself is mocked.

Firebase Cloud Messaging.

Committed source event/job, eligible recipient and preference, active device token, initialized Firebase Admin.

**Main success scenario:**
Worker atomically claims one due row, creates/updates notification record, sends to eligible tokens, records success and never repeats the same logical effect.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
No eligible recipient/token, opted-out preference, invalid/unregistered token, partial multicast failure, Firebase config absent, 429/5xx/timeout, worker crash before/after provider call, duplicate scheduler instances.

**Expected system behavior:**
Failure is recorded/retried according to job policy; invalid tokens are disabled/removed where implemented; source business transaction is never rolled back by asynchronous FCM failure.

Provider succeeds but DB status update fails, recipient state changes after enqueue, poison payload, clock skew, backfill horizon, outbox row locked by dead worker.

Multi-instance claim/retry with PostgreSQL and FCM emulator; partial token outcomes; crash-window idempotency; end-to-end payload navigation on mobile.

Atomic field-level projection with real DB under concurrent revoke; multi-group aggregation; notification deep-link after permission removal.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**

Member removed with open tasks, two actors complete simultaneously, task appears in unified today view, urgent alert preference override, note time ordering.

Task→today-view→FCM cross-module flow; member-removal reassignment; quick-note privacy; urgent alert payload/delivery through emulator.

## Content & Checklist

### Feature: Verified content lifecycle, search, approval, unpublish and public/mobile consumption

**Module:**
Content & Checklist

**Feature purpose:**
Create/version/review/approve/archive/unpublish verified articles and expose only eligible content through list/search/lifecycle endpoints.

**Actors / Roles:**
`CONTENT_ADMIN` author/editor; `SYSTEM_ADMIN` approver where defined; Mother/Family/Expert readers.

**Entry points:**
Admin content CRUD/version/decision/unpublish endpoints; reader `/api/v1/content...`; mobile verified-content screens; web content workspace.

**Integration flow:**
`Admin web -> AdminContent/Approval/Unpublish controllers -> sanitize/version/policy services -> content_items/audit -> PostgreSQL + Cloudinary image -> reader/search/lifecycle projection`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/AdminContentController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/ContentApprovalController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/ContentController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/service/ContentService.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/contentManagement/services/contentApi.ts`

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/integration/ContentIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/integration/ContentBodySanitizeIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/security/ContentSecurityTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/contentManagement/pages/ContentApprovalQueuePage.test.tsx`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/community/verified_content_detail_lifecycle_test.dart`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`content_items`, `audit_events`, attachment metadata; Cloudinary public images.

**External integrations:**
Cloudinary; Gemini RAG/AI moderation consumes approved content.

**Preconditions:**
Privileged role for mutation, valid sanitized body/topic/stage/version; approval state permits transition; reader JWT for content reads.

**Main success scenario:**
Draft/version persists, approval publishes one immutable approved version, readers/search/lifecycle return it, and unpublish/archive removes it from eligible reads while preserving history/audit.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Invalid/unsafe HTML, missing topic/stage/metadata, wrong role, stale/version immutable transition, target missing, duplicate decision, migrated template/content review required, image failure.

**Expected system behavior:**
Invalid lifecycle returns typed `ContentException` 400/404/409; unapproved/unpublished content is not returned to readers/RAG; sanitized body persists rather than raw unsafe markup.

**Important edge cases:**
Approval races edit, image orphan on rollback, content cited by AI then unpublished, search index/DB consistency, cache invalidation.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Content integration, sanitization, search, security and web/mobile tests are substantial; storage and Gemini consumers remain mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Full admin web→DB→reader mobile→RAG path; concurrent approve/edit/unpublish; storage compensation and search consistency.

**Module:**
Content & Checklist

**Feature purpose:**
Create/version/approve checklist templates and distribute eligible tasks to users/journeys with audit and migration compatibility.

**Actors / Roles:**
`CONTENT_ADMIN`; `SYSTEM_ADMIN` approver where required; system distribution jobs/services.

**Entry points:**
Admin checklist template/version/approval endpoints, sequence/distribution services and E2E attestation endpoint under its configured profile.

**Integration flow:**
`Admin web -> template controllers/services -> care_item_templates/audit -> PostgreSQL -> approval event/distribution -> checklist_instances/task_instances`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/AdminChecklistTemplateController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/controller/ChecklistTemplateApprovalController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/checklist/distribution/ChecklistDistributionService.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/checklist/sequence/ChecklistSequenceAdvanceService.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/contentManagement/pages/ChecklistFormPage.tsx`

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/content/integration/ChecklistTemplateAdminIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/checklist/ChecklistImportPostgresIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/checklist/distribution/ChecklistApprovalDistributionAuditContractTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/src/features/contentManagement/pages/ChecklistVersionHistoryPage.test.tsx`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`care_item_templates`, `checklist_instances`, `checklist_task_instances`, `audit_events`.

**External integrations:**
None required; notification jobs may be created after distribution.

**Preconditions:**
Valid privileged role, template metadata/stage/type/items, allowed version transition, eligible journey/user and distribution context.

**Main success scenario:**
Draft version persists, approval freezes it, distribution creates the correct instance/tasks exactly once, and user clients receive canonical task data.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Invalid template/item ordering/metadata, duplicate version/distribution, approved version immutable (`VERSION_IMMUTABLE`), migrated review required (`MIGRATION_REVIEW_REQUIRED`), stale approval, unauthorized role, incompatible lifecycle stage.

**Expected system behavior:**
Invalid/stale transitions return 400/409 and do not distribute. Required audit and distribution effects are atomic/idempotent according to service boundaries.

**Important edge cases:**
Approval and journey transition race, re-import legacy template, version activated with existing instances, partial distribution/audit failure, concurrent distributors.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Very large checklist backend suite includes many PostgreSQL, migration, distribution and audit tests; web template pages and one mobile integration flow exist.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Admin web approval through user mobile receipt with real DB; multi-instance distribution lock; notification/reminder side effects and rollback.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Item/task not found, system task mutation prohibited, invalid transition/action, duplicate command, permission revoked, stale/unsupported legacy item, recommendation profile missing/invalid, approved content unavailable.

Same task represented by reminder/checklist aliases, concurrent actions from Mother/Family, history retention, journey transition during rank, consent cleanup.

Cross-provider unified task E2E, concurrent mobile actions, permission revoke during action, recommendation API through lifecycle/consent change.

### Feature: Phone sensor, camera and location data boundaries

**Module:**
Expense & Device Data

**Feature purpose:**
Use phone IMU for fall detection, camera landmarks for exercise and location for map/safety while keeping monitoring explicit, disableable and purpose-bound.

**Actors / Roles:**
Mother; device OS/sensors; mobile app; backend safety/exercise/location services.

**Entry points:**
Flutter sensor/foreground/camera/location services and corresponding safety/exercise/location APIs.

**Integration flow:**
`Device permission/sensor -> Flutter processing -> derived event/landmarks/location only -> backend API -> PostgreSQL/provider`; raw continuous IMU streams are not intended for upload/storage.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/lib/features/safety/services/fall_detection_sensor_service.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/lib/features/safety/services/safety_foreground_service.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/lib/features/exercise/services/posture_event_streamer.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/location/controller/LocationSnapshotController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/entity/User.java` — implemented defaults keep fall detection and sensor permission disabled until explicitly enabled/granted.
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__init_schema.sql` — PostgreSQL defaults independently confirm the implemented opt-in boundary.

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/safety/fall_detection_sensor_service_test.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/safety/safety_foreground_coordinator_test.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/exercise/posture_event_streamer_test.dart`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/emergency/emergency_map_screen_test.dart`

**Database / Storage involved:**
Only derived safety/location/exercise observations/events should persist. No wearable or connected-medical-device table/interface was found.

**External integrations:**
Phone accelerometer/gyroscope, camera, geolocation and OS background service.

**Preconditions:**
Explicit permission, supported sensor/camera/location hardware and feature enablement; `User.fallDetectionEnabled` and the database column default to `false` in current code/migration.

**Main success scenario:**
Permissioned derived data drives the intended feature; disabling/revoking permission stops future collection and leaves a safe stopped/limited UI state.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Permission denied/revoked, unsupported sensor, background restriction, battery limitation, app killed/restarted, noisy/missing data, offline network, clock discontinuity.

**Expected system behavior:**
No raw continuous stream is uploaded when derived processing suffices. Denial preserves manual emergency guidance; unsupported capability does not fabricate measurements.

**Important edge cases:**
Permission changes mid-session, OS reboot, duplicate foreground task, sensor sampling burst, false-positive calibration, location consent independent of OS permission.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Mobile unit/widget tests cover services and coordinators; no physical-device automated integration suite was found.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Real Android/iOS device matrix, background/reboot/permission transitions, battery and unsupported-hardware behavior. No wearable/health-platform integration exists in code; whether that absence is intended Release 1 scope is **Needs manual verification** because the recent SRS files conflict.

**Module:**
Safety Alerts & FCM

**Feature purpose:**
Configure monitoring, verify sensor readiness, accept derived fall/impact signals and create canonical suspected-fall safety events.

**Actors / Roles:**
Mother; device sensors/mobile app; backend event listeners.

**Entry points:**
Safety-config, sensor-self-test and fall-detection endpoints; Flutter safety monitoring/self-test screens; config-change/fall listeners.

**Integration flow:**
`Flutter IMU detector -> FallDetectionController -> FallDetectionService -> safety_events/safety_monitoring_sessions -> PostgreSQL -> AFTER_COMMIT SuspectedFallDetectedHandler -> countdown/alert path`.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/safety/controller/FallDetectionController.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/safety/controller/SafetyConfigController.java`

**Related test files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/safety/FallDetectionControllerTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/safety/SensorSelfTestControllerTest.java`

**Database / Storage involved:**
`safety_events`, `safety_monitoring_sessions`, audit/notification state; derived diagnostics only.

**External integrations:**
Phone IMU and OS foreground task.

**Preconditions:**
Authenticated Mother, monitoring explicitly enabled, supported/permitted sensor, active monitoring session, valid derived signal.

**Main success scenario:**
Configuration and self-test establish readiness, derived suspected-fall signal persists exactly once and after-commit handler initiates countdown.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Monitoring disabled, self-test failed/stale, invalid signal/time, duplicate event, session not owned/inactive, sensor unavailable, concurrent start/stop/event.

**Expected system behavior:**
Disabled/unready monitoring does not create false active state; invalid signals return controlled error; duplicate/replayed signals do not create multiple countdowns.

**Important edge cases:**
Event during config disable, app process restart, clock skew, burst signals, false-positive threshold, after-commit handler failure.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Backend controller/service/migration/concurrency and mobile detector/diagnostic/UI tests are meaningful.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Physical device→API→real DB flow; background lifecycle; multi-signal deduplication with clock-controlled countdown.

**Main success scenario:**
Cancel/false-positive stops escalation; need-help or timeout transitions once to alert/emergency, persists evidence and sends eligible family notifications with urgent client navigation.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Event missing/foreign/already resolved, late or duplicate response, invalid transition, no family/token, partial FCM failure, job race, permission revoked, false-positive after irreversible alert.

**Expected system behavior:**
Only one terminal transition wins. Urgent UI remains visible without claiming dispatch. Delivery failure is retried/recorded and cannot erase safety evidence.

**Important edge cases:**
User response at exact countdown boundary, two job instances, FCM success then DB failure, multiple family tokens, offline user response, later false-positive analytics.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Countdown/listener/adapter and mobile sheet/contracts exist; provider and physical-device flow are mocked.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Clock-controlled device-to-family E2E with FCM emulator; terminal-state race matrix; partial delivery and replay; urgent deep-link authorization.

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/common/dev/DevDataSeederPostgresIntegrationTest.java`
- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/testsupport/HermeticDatasourceTestcontainersSmokeTest.java`

**Database / Storage involved:**
PostgreSQL schema managed by Flyway; all seeded domain tables.

**External integrations:**
Docker, Nginx, Cloudflare Tunnel; provider configuration/readiness boundaries.

**Preconditions:**
Correct deployment profile/secrets/network; DB reachable for readiness; `dev & !prod` plus `carebridge.dev-seed.enabled=true` for manual seed.

**Main success scenario:**
Readiness is anonymous only at the designated path and reflects DB availability; all other actuator operations are denied. Edge routes preserve auth/CORS/upload semantics. Dev seed is absent outside dev and creates deterministic data when enabled.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
DB unavailable, wrong profile/property, seed repeated/concurrent, unauthorized actuator path, invalid exact CORS origin, proxy upload limit/header mismatch, service unavailable, migration failure.

**Expected system behavior:**
Readiness returns healthy/unhealthy without exposing details beyond configuration; non-readiness actuator endpoints are denied; invalid CORS config fails startup; seed route does not exist outside dev.

**Important edge cases:**
Staging accidentally uses dev profile, Nginx body limit differs from Spring, forwarded scheme/origin, rolling migration with old app, health green while required external provider is disabled.

**Existing automated coverage:**
Readiness security/DB failure, dev seeder PostgreSQL and datasource smoke tests exist; edge topology is documented but not automated end-to-end.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Disposable full Docker edge stack, TLS/proxy/CORS/multipart and service-down tests; explicit assertion that dev seed bean/route is absent in staging/prod profiles; migration compatibility gate.

---

### Feature: Payment placeholder and absent wearable integration

**Module:**
Other / Unclassified

**Feature purpose:**
Document implementation absences that could otherwise be mistaken for working integrations; product release scope remains separate from code discovery.

**Actors / Roles:**
No actor can reach a payment or wearable integration in the current implementation.

**Entry points:**
No payment endpoint/controller implementation exists; no wearable/health-platform/connected-medical-device interface exists.

**Integration flow:**
No implemented runtime flow.

**Main source files:**

- `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/payment/controller/.gitkeep` — placeholder only.
- `02_Requirements/SRS/3_Functional_Specification_Detailed.md` — recent candidate scope says MF-13 connected-device integration is current release; not confirmed by code.
- `02_Requirements/SRS/3_Functional_Specification_Detailed_5_Non-Functional Requirements__6_Requirement Appendix.md` — recent draft says the opposite via `FS-06`/`NFR-DI-05`, and excludes paid processing via `NFR-CI-05`; scope conflict needs owner resolution.

**Related test files:**

No existing automated test found.

**Database / Storage involved:**
None implemented for payment/wearable data.

**External integrations:**
None implemented.

**Preconditions:**
Not applicable.

**Main success scenario:**
Not applicable to the current implementation; Release 1 intent is **Needs manual verification**.

**Validation / Negative scenarios:**
Any request claiming payment/wearable functionality is unsupported and needs product-scope verification rather than an invented test.

**Expected system behavior:**
No endpoint should be documented or tested as implemented. If future work enables it, a separate design and threat/privacy review is required.

**Important edge cases:**
Accidental UI exposure of placeholder routes; future schema addition without authorization/consent design.

**Existing automated coverage:**
None; this confirms implementation absence, not an approved product exclusion.

**Missing Integration Test coverage:**
Add a scope/route audit to prevent accidental exposure, and obtain an approved requirement decision before classifying wearable/payment coverage as intentionally out of scope.

# External Integration Inventory

`Failure Handling` records implemented behavior; entries marked **Needs manual verification** require a real provider sandbox or deployed environment because repository tests use a mock/stub boundary.

| Integration | Feature | Trigger | Request/Input | Expected Response/Effect | Failure Handling | Source Path | Test Path |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Firebase Authentication / Admin SDK | Federated registration, login and identity linking | Auth/link request supplies a Firebase-issued identity token | ID token and expected provider/audience | Verified provider identity is mapped or linked to a CareBridge user | Invalid proof, collision, unsupported provider and provider outage map to stable `AUTH-FED-*` responses; live Admin SDK verification needs sandbox confirmation | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/federation/FirebaseAdminTokenVerifier.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/integration/FederatedLoginIntegrationTest.java` |
| Firebase Realtime Database | Direct-chat conversation/call event signaling | A conversation message or call lifecycle event is persisted | Conversation, participants, event payload/version | Publisher writes an event consumable by authorized clients | Gateway absence/outage maps to direct-chat Firebase unavailable behavior; duplicate/event retention behavior is separately scheduled | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/integration/firebase/ConversationEventPublisherImpl.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/integration/firebase/ConversationEventPublisherImplTest.java` |
| Firebase Cloud Messaging | Normal notification delivery | Notification dispatch selects active device tokens | FCM message plus target token(s) | Device receives push and delivery attempt/state is recorded by notification flow | Per-token/provider failures are isolated; overload behavior is tested, but live delivery is **Needs manual verification** | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/notification/service/impl/FirebaseFcmServiceImpl.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/notification/service/impl/FcmServiceOverloadTest.java` |
| Firebase Cloud Messaging | Urgent family safety alert | Fall/countdown/emergency flow invokes family alert delivery | Alert data and eligible family device tokens | Urgent push is attempted for each eligible target and audit/delivery state remains observable | Missing/invalid tokens and provider failure must not corrupt the alert lifecycle; live high-priority semantics are **Needs manual verification** | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/emergency/service/impl/FamilyAlertService.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/emergency/FamilyAlertServiceTest.java` |
| Gemini API (Java RAG) | Evidence-grounded question answering | Authenticated RAG query reaches Gemini integration service | Query, approved context chunks and configured Gemini credentials/model | Parsed answer is returned with grounded context metadata | Invalid query/context count is rejected before call; no-Gemini startup and fallback/log redaction are tested | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/integration/gemini/client/GeminiHttpClient.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/integration/gemini/GeminiRagServiceStory69Test.java` |

| Gemini API (Python) | Triage conversational assistance | Triage service requests a model turn after deterministic safety gates | Canonical intake/turn context, evidence and configured model | Model output is normalized and deterministic rules remain authoritative | Triage continues through defined fallback behavior when Gemini is unavailable; provider timing/quotas need sandbox evidence | `05_Development/CareBridgeAITriageService/app/gemini_client.py` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAITriageService/tests/test_gemini_integration.py` |
