| SMTP / Gmail | OTP and transactional email | Registration/password/verification flow requests email | Recipient, subject/template data and mail configuration | Email is submitted to SMTP and challenge remains consistent | Send failure is surfaced by the security flow; deliverability, Gmail throttling and spam behavior are **Needs manual verification** | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/security/service/impl/GmailEmailService.java` | NONE — provider transport is not exercised by an isolated Gmail service integration test |
| Exercise-correction sidecar | Posture inference | Java exercise flow sends landmark frames | Exercise key, timestamp and landmark coordinates | Sidecar returns stage/score/correction result | Java adapter handles timeout/status; sidecar returns stable 400/422/503 envelopes and never exposes internal exception detail | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/main/java/com/carebridge/backend/exercise/inference/ExerciseCorrectionHttpAdapter.java` | `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/src/test/java/com/carebridge/backend/exercise/inference/ExerciseCorrectionHttpAdapterTest.java` |
| Device camera / sensors / location | Mobile posture, safety and nearby-facility flows | User grants permission and starts feature | Camera frames, accelerometer/sensor samples or coordinates | Client transforms device data and calls the matching backend/sidecar flow | Permission denial/unavailable hardware must remain a recoverable UI state; physical-device correctness is **Needs manual verification** | `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/lib/features/safety/services/safety_permission_service.dart` | `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/test/features/safety/safety_monitoring_screen_test.dart` |
| Nginx / Cloudflare edge | Public API routing, CORS and WebSocket upgrade | Client calls deployed API host | HTTP/WebSocket request, origin and forwarding headers | Request reaches backend with intended headers/timeouts; realtime upgrade is preserved | Edge rejects/rate-limits per configuration; deployed Cloudflare rules/certificates remain **Needs manual verification** | `05_Development/Deployment/nginx/edge-api.conf` | NONE — no edge smoke test was found |

# Integration Test Coverage Summary

The test-file counts below use one primary path-based module assignment so the 995 tracked executable test sources are not double-counted. A test can still cover multiple modules; the traceability matrix and detailed feature sections describe those cross-module effects. “Likely scenarios” is the count of concrete candidates in **Suggested Integration Test Cases**, not a claim that all are already automated.

| Module | Total features discovered | Existing automated test files (primary mapping) | Likely Integration Test scenarios | Major missing test areas | High-risk integration flows |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| Identity & Security | 4 | 159 | 8 | Full method/path/role/ownership matrix; real Firebase proof exchange; concurrent refresh/logout; consent/audit rollback | Authorization leakage, token replay, federated collision, privilege mutation without audit |
| Care Journeys | 5 | 61 | 6 | Cross-client journey transitions; canonical units/trends; screening-to-guidance; ML sidecar sandbox; vaccination/reminder race | Incorrect maternal/baby state, unsafe screening projection, duplicate/stale vaccine effects |
| Community & Moderation | 3 | 82 | 5 | PostgreSQL interaction concurrency; report claim/resolve/undo chain; malformed/live Gemini moderation | Anonymous-data leakage, double moderation, lost evidence, AI action without human control |
| Expert & Consultation | 4 | 87 | 6 | Full R2→CompreFace approval chain; location consent revocation; accept/reject/expiry race; Firebase/Zego sandbox | Unverified expert approval, multiple winning experts, context oversharing, duplicate chat/call effects |
| AI Triage & Gemini | 4 | 134 | 7 | Java↔Python deployed contract; provider timeout/429/malformed output; evidence withdrawal; memory consent deletion | RED downgrade/fail-open, parity drift, unsafe/fabricated citations, leaked health context |
| Emergency Map & TrackAsia | 2 | 17 | 3 | TrackAsia sandbox and malformed response; RED-to-session idempotency; partial-recipient FCM retry | Missing emergency escalation, fabricated route/ETA, repeated or lost alerts |
| Health Records & Storage | 2 | 40 | 3 | Field-level projection across roles; upload/DB compensation; current authorization on every file purpose | Health/file disclosure, orphaned private objects, stale access after revoke |
| Reminders & Notifications | 3 | 72 | 5 | Clock/timezone recurrence; unified command race; FCM token ownership transfer; worker crash after provider success | Duplicate/late tasks and pushes, cross-account token delivery, inconsistent outbox state |
| Family Sync | 3 | 41 | 3 | Invite single-use; revoke/read race across all shared resources; removal with assigned tasks | Cross-family disclosure, stale permissions, orphaned cooperative tasks |
| Content & Checklist | 3 | 150 | 4 | Approval/unpublish visibility across search/mobile/Gemini; one-time distribution/audit; recommendation privacy | Unreviewed content exposure, duplicate tasks, revoked content/evidence reuse |
| Expense & Device Data | 2 | 6 | 2 | Expense currency/summary contract; permission revocation during physical sensor/camera/location capture | Incorrect owner totals, continued collection after consent/OS permission loss |
| Safety Alerts & FCM | 2 | 25 | 3 | Device→API→countdown→family FCM E2E; exact clock boundary; authorized deep link | Missed fall escalation, duplicate terminal states, alert to unauthorized family |
| Other / Unclassified | 3 | 121 | 5 | Search privacy at scale; anonymous readiness outage; non-dev seed absence; supported-version migration; deployed edge | Hidden data in search, exposed seed route, schema loss, auth/CORS/header failure at edge |
| **Total** | **40** | **995** | **60** | The dominant gap is full-stack/provider-backed evidence, not absence of unit tests. | 17 of 20 registered risks score 6 or 9 and require mitigation evidence. |

## Highest Priority Integration Tests

| Rank | Test Case ID | Priority | Why it is high priority |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `ITS-SEC-008` | P0 | Exercises every externally callable Java method/path against role and authentication rules; the largest direct privacy/security surface. |
| 2 | `ITS-AI-001` | P0 | Proves deterministic RED safety rules cannot be downgraded by an unreliable or malicious model response. |
| 3 | `ITS-EMR-001` | P0 | Spans triage, emergency persistence, idempotency, family selection and alert publication for an urgent user outcome. |
| 4 | `ITS-SAFE-002` | P0 | Resolves a three-way cancel/need-help/scheduler race where duplicate or wrong terminal state can affect safety. |
| 5 | `ITS-FAM-002` | P0 | Verifies permission revocation atomically closes dashboard, health, calendar, task and file reads. |
| 6 | `ITS-SEC-004` | P0 | Protects session integrity against refresh-token replay and logout/refresh concurrency. |
| 7 | `ITS-OTH-004` | P0 | A migration failure can make every feature unavailable or silently damage production data. |
| 8 | `ITS-CON-001` | P0 | Prevents multiple experts from winning a consultation and creating inconsistent conversations. |
| 9 | `ITS-CHAT-001` | P0 | Couples durable idempotent message persistence to Firebase signaling and notification effects after commit. |
| 10 | `ITS-NOT-002` | P0 | Detects the classic provider-success/worker-crash window that otherwise duplicates user-visible notifications. |
| 11 | `ITS-FILE-002` | P0 | Ensures sensitive health, identity and chat objects cannot bypass current domain authorization through raw storage URLs. |
| 12 | `ITS-FILE-001` | P0 | Validates compensation when remote storage succeeds but the database transaction fails, preventing untracked private objects. |
| 13 | `ITS-AI-002` | P0 | Cross-language rule parity and pinned datasets are required for Java/Python to make the same safety decision. |
| 14 | `ITS-SEC-007` | P0 | Proves audit-required privileged mutations cannot commit without their append-only evidence. |
| 15 | `ITS-MOD-001` | P1 | Covers report creation, atomic moderator claim, resolution and evidence preservation across multiple components. |
| 16 | `ITS-CARE-005` | P1 | A vaccination update racing reminder dispatch can send medically stale guidance or duplicate an occurrence. |
| 17 | `ITS-AI-007` | P1 | Exercises timeout, throttling, 5xx, malformed output and invalid citations at the highest-change external boundary. |
| 18 | `ITS-EXP-001` | P1 | Validates the complete R2 document plus CompreFace decision chain before an Expert becomes eligible for approval. |
| 19 | `ITS-SAFE-003` | P1 | Confirms urgent FCM reaches only currently authorized family and opens an accessible event. |
| 20 | `ITS-OTH-005` | P1 | The deployed edge can break auth, CORS, multipart and failure semantics even when application-level tests pass. |

## Completeness Re-scan

- Re-scanned all tracked, project-owned implementation and test roots after the first pass. The final inventories cover 422 Java HTTP method/path rows (including both verbs on the shared reminder `skip` handler), 5 AI FastAPI routes, 2 exercise-sidecar FastAPI routes, 14 scheduled jobs and 14 valid event listeners.
- Reconciled every major implemented feature into 40 feature records and 40 source/test traceability rows; payment and wearable entries record current implementation absence without treating conflicting SRS scope as authoritative.
- Audited all four tracked SRS files against Git history. Their August 5–6 commits make them possibly recent, but 47 later commits (41 in implementation) and the connected-device contradiction prevent using them as current pass/fail oracles without owner approval.
- Inspected and cataloged 995 executable test source files individually: 721 backend Java, 36 web, 163 mobile, 50 AI-triage pytest, 16 AI-evaluation pytest, 1 Firebase rules test and 8 PowerShell automation/deployment runners.
- Cataloged 74 project-owned test resources/fixtures, including PostgreSQL migrations/seeds, parity/evaluation datasets and checksums, Flutter golden oracles, Promptfoo suites and test bootstrap configuration.
- Rechecked controller/router mappings, services/policies, repositories/entities, migrations, security configuration, provider adapters, events/jobs, client route/API layers and tracked runtime/deployment configuration. Exact path existence and required section checks are reported by the final static verification pass.

## Directories and Files Not Semantically Analyzed

The following were intentionally not semantically analyzed; this does not omit project-owned executable source:

- Dependency/generated/cache roots excluded by the requested scope: `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/node_modules`, `05_Development/CareBridgeWebApp/dist`, `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/build`, `05_Development/CareBridgeMobileApp/.dart_tool`, `05_Development/CareBridgeAPI/target`, `05_Development/CareBridgeAITriageService/.venv`, Python `__pycache__`, `06_Testing/CareBridgeAIEvaluation/.pytest_cache`, `06_Testing/CareBridgeAIEvaluation/.ruff_cache`, and `.git`.
- Historical generated run payloads under `06_Testing/TestResults` were inventoried as evidence outputs but not treated as current executable coverage; they cannot establish that the merged repository is green today.
- Binary UI/reference artifacts (600 tracked binary files, mostly screenshots and platform assets) were path-inventoried but not OCR/visually reviewed: principally `03_Design/UI_UX/MobileAppScreen`, `03_Design/UI_UX/WebAppScreen`, diagram exports, Flutter platform icons/assets, ML model binaries and the three Flutter golden PNGs. Their consuming tests/source were analyzed.
- Office/archive/reference binaries were not parsed semantically: `01_Planning/ProgressReports/Project Weekly Report_Group79.xlsx`, `01_Planning/ProgressReports/Task Project - SEP490-G79-CareBridge.xlsx`, `06_Testing/AcceptanceTest/SEP490_G127_Report5_Acceptance_Test.zip`, `06_Testing/UnitTesting/Report5.1_Unit Test_reference1.xlsx`, `06_Testing/UnitTesting/Report5.1_UnitTest_reference2.xlsx`, `06_Testing/UnitTesting/Report5_Unit Test_CareBridge.xlsx`, `06_Testing/UnitTesting/Report5_Unit Test_template.xls`, `07_Reports/Report4_Software Design Document.docx`, `08_References/Template/EDS_TEMPLATE_V2.0.pdf`, and `08_References/Template/TDD_TEMPLATE_V1.pdf`. Text/Markdown implementations, exported requirements and executable tests were used instead.
- Secret values in `.env` and tracked runtime configuration were deliberately not reproduced; only configuration keys, enablement behavior and the presence of an exposed-secret risk were analyzed.

Repository scan completed.
